# Agents · I · part 3

959 entries, implementation-analyzer to implementer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **implementation-analyzer**: Analyzes codebase implementation details. Call the implementation-analyzer agent when you need to find detailed information about specific components. As always, the more detailed your request prompt, the better! ([samosunaz/agent-skills](https://github.com/samosunaz/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/samuel/agents/implementation-analyzer.md))
- **implementation-architect**: Narrows a broad plan to the next smallest safe implementation slice. Use when: architecture-sensitive changes, migration sequencing, cross-subsystem refactors, shared state changes, or any task where over-scoping is likely. ([Chulf58/FORGE](https://github.com/Chulf58/FORGE/blob/HEAD/agents/implementation-architect.md))
- **implementation-architect**: DevRev dashboard and widget engineer. Invoked when building widget JSON configs, generating dashboard layouts, fixing broken dashboard metrics, debugging SQL in widgets, or configuring any DevRev visualization (table, chart, metric, heatmap). Handles both creation (generate JSON for widget-preview) and fix mode (fetch existing JSON via widgets.get API, diagnose, produce corrected JSON). Invoke when the conversation involves DevRev widget JSON, dashboard-preview, widget-preview, Oasis data sources, SQL for analytics, visualization configuration, or any "build/fix the dashboard" request. ([QK-SnapIn/devrev-qk-agents](https://github.com/QK-SnapIn/devrev-qk-agents/blob/HEAD/devrev-agents/agents/implementation-architect.md))

- **implementer**: Implementa un **plan aprobado** ejecutándolo **fase a fase**. Es el eslabón entre `planner` y `qa`: convierte `improvement-plan.md` + `tasks.md` en **código funcionando**. ([daycry/custom-agents](https://github.com/daycry/custom-agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implementa un plan aprobado ejecutándolo fase a fase. Lee el `improvement-plan.md` y el `tasks.md` de una iniciativa en `docs/roadmap/&lt;fecha&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;/`, escribe el código real del proyecto para cumplir cada tarea (T-XX) y sus criterios de aceptación, y mantiene `tasks.md` como **ledger canónico** marcando el estado de cada tarea (checkbox + estado) a medida que avanza. Trabaja sobre una rama, respeta los guardrails del repo y hace handoff a `qa` al terminar. Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "implementa el plan", "ejecuta las tareas", "desarrolla el roadmap", "implementa la fase X". ([daycry/custom-agents](https://github.com/daycry/custom-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Implements a clearly specified, bounded coding task (a module, its tests, wiring, content entries) and proves it with the project's local checks. Use when the design is decided and the work is independent and large: spec'd edits across many files, or a long build you don't need to watch. Give it the goal, files, rules and acceptance criteria. Not when the task is small (a one-line fix), when each step needs the last one's result, or when it needs a design decision. Does not commit or push. ([DdraigFawr13/adventure-manager](https://github.com/DdraigFawr13/adventure-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementer.md) · ★1 · 11 copies)
- **implementer**: Implements features, writes tests, and commits code per the implementation plan. Escalates deviations — never deviates silently. Used by Team Dispatch. ([denormans/claude-code-workflow](https://github.com/denormans/claude-code-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: brief와 티켓 하나를 받아 명세가 분명한 기능을 구현하고 완료 기준을 확인한다. 구현 티켓을 처음 맡길 때 쓴다. ([develeep/dev-gongbang](https://github.com/develeep/dev-gongbang/blob/HEAD/extras/omp/agents/implementer.md) · ★1)
- **implementer**: Non-interactive implementation worker dispatched by oc_run or the orch supervisor: edits the repository, runs checks, commits and pushes its branch. Session profile: claude --agent implementer, or the agent a command pins; never chosen for ad-hoc delegation.
